#### 公社政治 ####
Cof_politics = { 
	Cof_voting_time_for_bill = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { has_country_flag = voting_for_bill }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 14
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = bill_agree_vote_count
			bill_to_judge = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = current_bill_content
			effect_tooltip = { bill_pass_effect = yes }
		}
	}

	Cof_finish_voting_ahead = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		allowed = { tag = FRA }
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = voting_can_be_finished_ahead
			has_country_flag = voting_for_bill
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = bill_pass
				if = {
       				limit = { has_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434 }
					check_variable = { agree_vote > 434 }
				}
				else = { check_variable = { agree_vote > 326 } }
			}
		}
		cost = 20
		complete_effect = {
			add_days_mission_timeout = {
				mission = Cof_voting_time_for_bill
				days = -14
			}
		}
	}
	#大选
	Cof_election = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { has_country_flag = election_time }
		available = { always = no }
		is_good = yes
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = Cof_election_tt
			clr_country_flag = election_time
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { seat_synd > seat_jaco }
						check_variable = { seat_synd > seat_oppo }
						check_variable = { seat_synd > seat_sore }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = cof_1941_election_start }
						country_event = cofpolitics.56
					}
					else = { country_event = cofpolitics.121 }
				}
				
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { seat_oppo > seat_jaco }
						check_variable = { seat_oppo > seat_synd }
						check_variable = { seat_oppo > seat_sore }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = cof_1941_election_start }
						country_event = cofpolitics.57
					}
					else = { country_event = cofpolitics.122 }
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { seat_jaco > seat_synd }
						check_variable = { seat_jaco > seat_oppo }
						check_variable = { seat_jaco > seat_sore }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = cof_1941_election_start }
						country_event = cofpolitics.58
					}
					else = { country_event = cofpolitics.123 }
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { seat_sore > seat_jaco }
						check_variable = { seat_sore > seat_oppo }
						check_variable = { seat_sore > seat_synd }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { has_country_flag = cof_1941_election_start }
						country_event = cofpolitics.59
					}
					else = { country_event = cofpolitics.124 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	Cof_election_synd_mode1 = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		visible = { has_country_flag = election_time }
		allowed = { tag = FRA }
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = election_decision_times_less_than_4
				check_variable = { election_decision_times < 4 }
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { election_decision_times = 1 }

			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
			set_temp_variable = { smc = 8 }
			set_temp_variable = { src = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { occ = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { oac = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { jnc = -4 }
			set_temp_variable = { jrc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { soc = -4 }
			set_temp_variable = { syc = 0 }
			bgt_seat_change = yes
		}
	}

	Cof_enemy_at_the_gates_mission = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { always = no }
		available = {
			OR = {
				has_idea = bill_ten_year_model
				has_completed_focus = FRA_post_war_democracy_commitments
			}
		}
		is_good = yes
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 30
		complete_effect = {
			add_political_power = 50
			add_stability = 0.05
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -100
			add_stability = -0.1

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_society
			set_temp_variable = { wc = -4 }
       		set_temp_variable = { pc = -4 } 
	        set_temp_variable = { mc = -4 }
    		set_temp_variable = { sc = -4 }
        	set_temp_variable = { bc = -4 }
        	set_temp_variable = { ac = -4 }
	        class_pop_satisfactions_change = yes
		}
	}

#CSP采纳意见
# CCSP
	CCSP_suggestion_synd_mode = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		visible = { always = no }
		allowed = { tag = FRA }
		days_remove = 180
		fire_only_once = yes
		modifier = { 
			monthly_change_bgt_vs_csp = 1
			political_power_factor = -0.2
			economy_power_factor = -0.2
		}
		remove_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = CSP_suggestion_CCSP
			set_temp_variable = { t = CSP_budget_CCSP }
			multiply_temp_variable = { t = 0.5 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { t = 0.5 }

			set_temp_variable = { gainpp = 50 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { gainpp = t }
			add_political_power = gainpp

			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
			set_temp_variable = { iosc = -5 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { iosc = t }
	        intensity_of_struggle_change = yes

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_society
			set_temp_variable = { wc = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { pc = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { mc = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { sc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { bc = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { ac = 0 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { wc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { pc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { mc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { sc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { bc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { ac = t }
			class_pop_satisfactions_change = yes

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_economy
			set_temp_variable = { epc = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { evc = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { eibc = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { eipc = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { eiac = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { eamc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { eaec = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { eaac = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { eqc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { elc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { eic = 0 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { epc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { evc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eibc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eipc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eiac = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eamc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eaec = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eaac = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eqc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { elc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { eic = t }
			cof_econ_target_change = yes

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_military
			set_temp_variable = { atimpc = 5 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp1c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp2c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp3c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp4c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp5c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp6c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp7c = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { atimp8c = 0 }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimpc = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp1c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp2c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp3c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp4c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp5c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp6c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp7c = t }
			multiply_temp_variable = { atimp8c = t }
			Cof_army_theory_implementation_change = yes

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			set_temp_variable = { aac = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { awc = 2 }

	Cof_syndmode_tent_mission = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { has_country_flag = under_tent_POU_threat }
		available = { 
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = tent_more_than_two
				check_variable = { under_tent_number > 1 }
			}
		}
		is_good = no
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 730
		complete_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = under_tent_successed
			country_event = cofpolitics.191
			clr_country_flag = under_tent_POU_threat
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = if_tent_failed
			country_event = cofpolitics.190
		}
	}

	Cof_syndmode_benefit_sorelian = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		allowed = { tag = FRA }
		visible = { 
			NOT = { has_country_flag = synd_mode_politics_landscape_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = under_tent_sorelian_failed }
			has_completed_focus = FRA_to_be_change_unity_first 
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_anarchist }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_technocrat }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_communist }
		}
		available = { 
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_anarchist }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_technocrat }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_communist }
		}
		days_remove = 60
		custom_cost_trigger = { has_army_experience > 4.99 }
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_ArmXP_5
		modifier = { experience_gain_army_factor = 0.05 }
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_sorelian
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = under_tent_sorelian }
				set_temp_variable = { ssc = 2 }
			}
			else = { set_temp_variable = { ssc = 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { asc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { tsc = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { csc = 0 }
			syndmode_sincerity_change = yes
		}
		remove_effect = { clr_country_flag = syndmode_benefit_sorelian }
	}

	Cof_syndmode_bring_in_technocrat_test_of_sincerity = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { 
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { technocrat_target_sincerity > 0 } }
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = technocrat_test_of_sincerity_trigger
					OR = {
						check_variable = { technocrat_sincerity = technocrat_target_sincerity }
						check_variable = { technocrat_sincerity > technocrat_target_sincerity }
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = { 
				limit = { has_country_flag = syndmode_technocrat_babeltower }
				has_idea = bill_local_committee_reform
				has_idea = bill_local_council_reform
			}
		}
		is_good = no
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 360
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = syndmode_technocrat_babeltower }
				weak_central_government_improve = yes
			}
			set_country_flag = under_tent_technocrat
			under_tent_number_judge = yes
			under_the_tent_idea_change = yes
			country_event = cofpolitics.163

			effect_tooltip = {
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = Cof_under_the_tent_0000
					add_idea = Cof_under_the_tent_0010
				}
			}
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			country_event = cofpolitics.164
		}
	}

#工农派议程
	Cof_oppounit_cgt_reform_mission = {
		icon = ger_mefo_bills
		activation = { always = no }
		available = { 
			has_completed_focus = FRA_weaken_CGT
			OR = {
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = bill_has_passed
						BILL = bill_CGT_pluralistic
					}
					OR = {
						has_idea = bill_CGT_pluralistic
						has_idea = bill_CGT_pluralistic_agri
						has_idea = bill_CGT_pluralistic_culture
						has_idea = bill_CGT_pluralistic_all
					}
				}	
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = {
						localization_key = bill_has_passed
						BILL = bill_CGT_depoliticized
					}
					OR = {
						has_idea = bill_CGT_depoliticized
						has_idea = bill_CGT_depoliticized_update
					}	
				}
			}
		}
		is_good = no
		fixed_random_seed = no
		days_mission_timeout = 360
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = PCOP_AFA_problem_CGT_successed
			add_to_variable = {
				PCOP_AFA_unity = 30
				tooltip = PCOP_AFA_unity_change_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { seat_oppo_comm > seat_oppo_anar } }
				add_to_variable = { 
					PCOP_AFA_union = 2
					tooltip = PCOP_AFA_union_add_PCOP_2
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { seat_oppo_comm < seat_oppo_anar } }
				add_to_variable = { 
					PCOP_AFA_union = -2 
					tooltip = PCOP_AFA_union_add_AFA_2
				}
			}
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			set_country_flag = PCOP_AFA_problem_CGT_failed
			add_to_variable = {
				PCOP_AFA_unity = -50
				tooltip = PCOP_AFA_unity_change_tt
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { seat_oppo_comm > seat_oppo_anar } }
				add_to_variable = { 
					PCOP_AFA_union = -3
					tooltip = PCOP_AFA_union_add_AFA_3
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { seat_oppo_comm < seat_oppo_anar } }
				add_to_variable = { 
					PCOP_AFA_union = 3
					tooltip = PCOP_AFA_union_add_PCOP_3
				}
			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = PCOP_AFA_union_break_warning
		}
	}
